title: "2026-08-02 - How rogue officers turned a nationwide camera network into a tool for stalking - washingtonpost.com"
source: "The Washington Post"

published: 2026-08-02
created: 2026-08-14
description: "Flock’s array of license-plate cameras was built to fight crime. But at least 50 law enforcement officers were charged with or accused of misusing it and other systems."
tags:
  - "clippings"

---
33 min

JEFFERSON, Ga. — Marci Bakely couldn’t understand how her ex-boyfriend always seemed to know where she was.

When she went to buy groceries or on a date or to the doctor, he’d text her about it, sometimes within minutes, despite working 15 miles from her home as a police chief in a small town in the Atlanta suburbs. One day in 2024, when he incorrectly suspected that she’d gone to see a man against his wishes, he messaged her, “I didn’t think you would slip up,” according to texts she shared with The Washington Post.

data-contentid="OU3MCUCCTNFVDPK5SAZNWFM6T4" data-el="text" class="wpds-c-heFNVF wpds-c-heFNVF-iPJLV-css overrideStyles font-copy" dir="null">When she went to buy groceries or on a date or to the doctor, he’d text her about it, sometimes within minutes, despite working 15 miles from her home as a police chief in a small town in the Atlanta suburbs. One day in 2024, when he incorrectly suspected that she’d gone to see a man against his wishes, he messaged her, “I didn’t think you would slip up,” according to texts she shared with The Washington Post. </p></div></div><div></div></div><div class="wpds-c-gVGMge wpds-c-gVGMge-ijVZkQg-css article-footer"><hr class="wpds-c-czFuUx"><div class="wpds-c-dhzjXW wpds-c-dhzjXW-ivSGRq-css footer-sm overrideStyles test article-bottom-action-bar"><div class="wpds-c-rPmfy"><p class="wpds-c-esEUdQ wpds-c-esEUdQ-hyvuql-isBold-true wpds-c-esEUdQ-igDDRwa-css headingStyleOverrides" data-apitype="header" data-contentid="0" data-testid="article-header">What readers are saying</p><div class="wpds-c-bHXelH wpds-c-bHXelH-erqBZw-maxWidth-md wpds-c-bHXelH-ijLYHBl-css"><div class="wpds-c-bHXelH wpds-c-bHXelH-erqBZw-maxWidth-md wpds-c-bHXelH-iiSpORC-css"><div class="wpds-c-bHXelH wpds-c-bHXelH-erqBZw-maxWidth-md wpds-c-bHXelH-igoXVOL-css"><div>The comments on the article express significant concerns about the misuse of Flock's surveillance technology, particularly by law enforcement officers.
